id: toolu_01EYFTm9QdiHsVQZbc7FMj98
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/agent_runner.go",
  "old_string": "func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {\n\t// Build command: gemini -m <model> -p \"<prompt>\" --yolo --allowed-tools <tools>\n\t// --yolo auto-approves all tool use (required for non-interactive mode)\n\t// --allowed-tools restricts which tools can run without confirmation\n\targs := []string{\n\t\t\"-m\", r.Model,\n\t\t\"-p\", prompt,\n\t\t\"--yolo\", // Auto-approve all actions for non-interactive mode\n\t}\n\n\tif len(tools) > 0 {\n\t\targs = append(args, \"--allowed-tools\")\n\t\targs = append(args, tools...)\n\t}",
  "new_string": "func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {\n\t// Build command: gemini -m <model> -p \"<prompt>\" --approval-mode auto_edit --allowed-tools <tools>\n\t// --approval-mode auto_edit: auto-approves edit tools (write_file, replace) while prompting for others\n\t// --allowed-tools: comma-separated list of tools that bypass confirmation (e.g., git commands)\n\targs := []string{\n\t\t\"-m\", r.Model,\n\t\t\"-p\", prompt,\n\t\t\"--approval-mode\", \"auto_edit\",\n\t}\n\n\t// Add default git tools that should be allowed without confirmation\n\tdefaultAllowedTools := []string{\n\t\t\"ShellTool(git status)\",\n\t\t\"ShellTool(git add)\",\n\t\t\"ShellTool(git commit)\",\n\t\t\"ShellTool(git diff)\",\n\t\t\"ShellTool(git log)\",\n\t}\n\n\t// Merge with any additional tools passed in\n\tallTools := append(defaultAllowedTools, tools...)\n\tif len(allTools) > 0 {\n\t\targs = append(args, \"--allowed-tools\", strings.Join(allTools, \",\"))\n\t}"
}
```

```
   266→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   267→	// Build command: gemini -m <model> -p "<prompt>" --approval-mode auto_edit --allowed-tools <tools>
   268→	// --approval-mode auto_edit: auto-approves edit tools (write_file, replace) while prompting for others
   269→	// --allowed-tools: comma-separated list of tools that bypass confirmation (e.g., git commands)
   270→	args := []string{
   271→		"-m", r.Model,
   272→		"-p", prompt,
   273→		"--approval-mode", "auto_edit",
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Add default git tools that should be allowed without confirmation
   277→	defaultAllowedTools := []string{
   278→		"ShellTool(git status)",
   279→		"ShellTool(git add)",
   280→		"ShellTool(git commit)",
   281→		"ShellTool(git diff)",
   282→		"ShellTool(git log)",
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// Merge with any additional tools passed in
   286→	allTools := append(defaultAllowedTools, tools...)
   287→	if len(allTools) > 0 {
   288→		args = append(args, "--allowed-tools", strings.Join(allTools, ","))
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	// Create context with timeout
   292→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   293→	defer cancel()
   294→
   295→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   296→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", args...)
   297→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   298→
   299→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   300→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   301→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   302→
   303→	start := time.Now()
   304→	err := cmd.Run()
   305→	duration := time.Since(start)
   306→
   307→	result := &AgentResult{
   308→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   309→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
```

> AGENT

The Gemini CLI runner is now working. Here's a summary of the changes:

**Changes made to e2e tests:**

1. **`agent_runner.go`**: Implemented `GeminiCLIRunner` with:
   - `--approval-mode auto_edit` for auto-approving file edits
   - `--allowed-tools` with git commands that bypass confirmation
   - Model selection via `E2E_GEMINI_MODEL` env var (default: `gemini-2.5-flash`)

2. **`testenv.go`**: 
   - Added error logging when agent fails (shows stdout/stderr)
   - Made JSONL transcript validation agent-aware (only enforced for Claude Code)

**Usage:**

```bash
# Run with Gemini (use -parallel 1 to avoid rate limiting)
E2E_AGENT=gemini go test -tags=e2e -parallel 1 -timeout=30m -v ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...

# Run specific tests to avoid rate limit issues
E2E_AGENT=gemini go test -tags=e2e -parallel 1 -v -run "TestE2E_BasicWorkflow|TestE2E_CheckpointMetadata" ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...

# With different model
E2E_AGENT=gemini E2E_GEMINI_MODEL=gemini-2.5-pro go test -tags=e2e -parallel 1 -v ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...
```

**Caveats:**
- Gemini free tier has rate limits (60 req/min, 1000 req/day) - use `-parallel 1`
- Tests take longer with Gemini due to rate limit backoff/retry
- Transcript format differs from Claude Code (not JSONL), so JSON validation is skipped for Gemini

**Gemini CLI e2e tests are now working.** Changes made:

1. **`agent_runner.go`**: Full Gemini CLI implementation with `--approval-mode auto_edit` and `--allowed-tools`

2. **`testenv.go`**:

**Usage:**
```bash
# Run with Gemini (use -parallel 1 to avoid rate limiting)
E2E_AGENT=gemini go test -tags=e2e -parallel 1 -timeout=30m -v ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...

# Run specific tests
E2E_AGENT=gemini go test -tags=e2e -parallel 1 -v -run "TestE2E_BasicWorkflow" ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...
```

**Test results with Gemini:**
- Most tests pass when run sequentially (`-parallel 1`)
- Some tests may timeout due to Gemini free tier rate limits (60 req/min)

```
    52→# Create temp files with proper extensions (works on both Linux and macOS)
    53→tmpdir=$(mktemp -d)
```
